EMP1 ** WS-NEG-TERM.\n           COMPUTE WS-TEMP3 ROUNDED = 1 - WS-TEMP2.\n\n           IF WS-TEMP3 = 0\n               MOVE WS-LOAN-NUM TO WS-MONTHLY-PAYMENT\n
           ELSE\n               COMPUTE WS-MONTHLY-PAYMENT ROUNDED =\n                   WS-LOAN-NUM * WS-MONTHLY-RATE / WS-TEMP3\n           END-IF.\n\n
